**THE BUG (T128_uneven_plen REFERENCE abort) — RESOLVED as a misattribution (2026-09-03).**
**This is NOT a single-seq bug.** The abort "append left a hole (pool=mtp pos=125 slot=61
 tail=59)" was the **PRE-fix BATCHED `any_draft`-starvation abort**, mislabeled as
"reference run failed" because the batched phase runs first in a `NINFER_SEQ_ONLY` invocation
(docs/135 §5). The batched fix `0257878c` (merged at `6857c7ea`) resolves it. Verified on the
merged state (GPU, window 103): the exact cell `--tokens 128 --mtp 3` completes RC=0 —
`[batched] 2 lanes finished` + `[SEQ-ONLY] dumped single-seq MTP + plain for both lanes`, 4 seq
dumps at 128 tokens each, and the R5 trace is clean (reaches F=125 at r036 then F=128/r037 and
completes — no hole). `tools/ops/pool_frontier_replay` (CPU) also reports the round pattern is
SELF-CONSISTENT. See docs/137. The pre-fix "MTP verify advances by VERIFIED span vs cur_F by
a+1 / rewind never extends" explanation was the pre-fix hypothesis and is a misattribution.
Full pin: docs/130 §9.8 (wo/kv-uniform-ci-gate, a49ae842). No single-seq fix needed; re-run the
T2.1 / full MTP suite on the merged state to confirm GREEN.

**CROSS-COMPARE (coordinator-verified, the primary route to the intended semantics):**
the single-seq MTP path is UNCHANGED between the pre-batched baseline `8a5f42a8`
(parent of the batched-MTP commit `19f864b4`) and current HEAD. Batched work only ADDED
`kvarn_attend_mtp_batched` + rerouted. So the single-seq reference runs the ORIGINAL code
⇒ bug is PRE-EXISTING, not a batched regression. Original single-seq `kvarn_attend_mtp`
(text_context_impl.h:714) + `gqa_kv_append_kvarn_and_commit` (kvarn_workspace.cpp:580)
are the reference for intended pool-advance semantics.

**CACHE-TYPE SCOPE (confirmed):** the ABORT is KVARN_K4V2-specific. The hole is a
tile/page/slot invariant in `gqa_kv_append_kvarn_batched` (kvarn_workspace.cpp:402) —
fires when appended positions are non-contiguous in a tile (slot != tail_count). Both
`kvarn_attend_mtp` (single-seq) and `kvarn_attend_mtp_batched` REQUIRE KVARN_K4V2 (throw
otherwise). General BF16 `gqa_kv_append` (gqa_attention_prefill.cu) has NO such check
(silent mis-write, no abort). ROOT CAUSE (non-contiguous positions from MTP-verify span
accounting) = cache-agnostic MTP-pipeline bug.

## AGENT-COMM PLAYBOOK (2026-09-07, coordinator — verified working, do NOT re-derive)

**ROUTING (permanent):** A1/A2 (pi sessions) → **intercom** tool. Gemini (antigravity 256adaf6) → **agent_comm** tool (broker localhost:3421). `agent_comms` (the room/DM-flavored twin) is DEAD: `dm`, `list_rooms`, `create_room` return "Unknown action"/field errors — never use it for delivery (its `agents` view mirrors agent_comm's registry, read-only use only).

**agent_comm (verified this session):**
- `agents` → roster with `last_heartbeat` (keeps updating even when idle — NOT proof of work) and `last_activity` (the only proof the agent processed anything).
- `send` → `to=<name>` AND content — exactly ONE of `to` or `channel`; specifying both = VALIDATION_ERROR "Cannot specify both \"to\" and \"channel\"".
- `inbox`/`poll`/`state`/`channels`/`register` — reported by the tool schema; verify on first use, do not assume.

**DELIVERY VERIFICATION PROTOCOL (mandatory after every send):** wait 1-2 min → `agents` → check recipient's `last_activity`. Advanced = delivered + processed. NOT advanced = message sat in the queue (harness not polling) → RE-SEND once with "ACK REQUIRED + deadline". Still dead → escalate to the user (user wakes the session directly; coordinator cannot wake an antigravity session from the mesh).

**WAKE PROTOCOL (idle agents):** message must contain: explicit ACK REQUIRED, the concrete next step, and a deadline ("reply with ACK + plan within 15 minutes"). Heartbeat updates while idle — never cite heartbeat as activity.

**INTERCOM (A1/A2, verified):** `send` to=<name|short-id|full-id> + message = plain message. `reply` works ONLY against a PENDING ASK (agent's message sent as ask); against a plain send it fails "Reply target does not match a pending ask" → use `send`. `pending` = list unresolved inbound asks. `list` = status (thinking/idle/tool) + ctx% — the idle-push trigger (user directive: ANY idle status = immediate wake, no exceptions).

**LESSON (2026-09-07):** the 14:00:42Z gemini brief went out via agent_comm and the broker ACCEPTED it (id 254) — but gemini's last_activity never moved (13:45:52) and he "did nothing all this time" because no verification step followed the send. Acceptance ≠ delivery. The re-fire on `agent_comm send to=gemini` (id 255) at 14:25:02Z is the canonical channel; verify via last_activity before believing anything is in flight.
